For purposes of this subdivision "durable medical equipment," "prosthetic device," "Medicare," and "Medicaid" have the definitions given in subdivision 7.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Clothing.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Clothing is exempt. For purposes of this subdivision, "clothing" means all human wearing apparel suitable for general use.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Clothing includes, but is not limited to, aprons, household and shop; athletic supporters; baby receiving blankets; bathing suits and caps; beach capes and coats; belts and suspenders; boots; coats and jackets; costumes; children and adult diapers, including disposable; ear muffs; footlets; formal wear; garters and garter belts; girdles; gloves and mittens for general use; hats and caps; hosiery; insoles for shoes; lab coats; neckties; overshoes; pantyhose; rainwear; rubber pants; sandals; scarves; shoes and shoe laces; slippers; sneakers; socks and stockings; steel-toed boots; underwear; uniforms, athletic and nonathletic; and wedding apparel.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Clothing does not include the following:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) belt buckles sold separately;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) costume masks sold separately;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) patches and emblems sold separately;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) sewing equipment and supplies, including but not limited to, knitting needles, patterns, pins, scissors, sewing machines, sewing needles, tape measures, and thimbles;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) sewing materials that become part of clothing, including but not limited to, buttons, fabric, lace, thread, yarn, and zippers;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) clothing accessories or equipment;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) sports or recreational equipment;</p>
          <p class="in">(8) protective equipment; and</p>
          <p class="in">(9) fur clothing as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.61#stat.297A.61.46">297A.61, subdivision 46</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">For purposes of this subdivision, "clothing accessories or equipment" means incidental items worn on the person or in conjunction with clothing. Clothing accessories and equipment include, but are not limited to, briefcases; cosmetics; hair notions, including barrettes, hair bows, and hairnets; handbags; handkerchiefs; jewelry; nonprescription sunglasses; umbrellas; wallets; watches; and wigs and hairpieces. "Sports or recreational equipment" means items designed for human use and worn in conjunction with an athletic or recreational activity that are not suitable for general use. Sports and recreational equipment includes, but is not limited to, ballet and tap shoes; cleated or spiked athletic shoes; gloves, including, but not limited to, baseball, bowling, boxing, hockey, and golf gloves; goggles; hand and elbow guards; life preservers and vests; mouth guards; roller and ice skates; shin guards; shoulder pads; ski boots; waders; and wetsuits and fins. "Protective equipment" means items for human wear and designed as protection of the wearer against injury or disease or as protection against damage or injury of other persons or property but not suitable for general use. Protective equipment includes, but is not limited to, breathing masks; clean room apparel and equipment; ear and hearing protectors; face shields; finger guards; hard hats; helmets; paint or dust respirators; protective gloves; safety glasses and goggles; safety belts; tool belts; and welders gloves and masks.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Baby products.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Breast pumps, baby bottles and nipples, pacifiers, teething rings, and infant syringes are exempt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Caskets; vaults.</span></h2>
          <p>Caskets and burial vaults for human burial are exempt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Automobiles; veterans with a disability.</span></h2>
          <p>Automobiles or other conveyances are exempt if the purchaser is assisted by a grant from the United States in accordance with United States Code, title 38, section 3902.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Parts and accessories used to make a motor vehicle accessible to a person with a disability.</span></h2>
          <p>Parts, accessories, and labor charges that are used solely to modify a motor vehicle to make it accessible to persons with a disability are exempt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Textbooks.</span></h2>
          <p>Textbooks, including digital books, that are prescribed for use in conjunction with a course of study in a school, college, university, and private career school to students who are regularly enrolled at such institutions are exempt. For purposes of this subdivision (1) a "school" is as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120A.22#stat.120A.22.4">120A.22, subdivision 4</a>; and (2) "private career school" means a school licensed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/136A.822">136A.822</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.13a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13a.<span class="headnote">Instructional materials.</span></h2>
          <p>Instructional materials, other than textbooks, that are prescribed for use in conjunction with a course of study in a postsecondary school, college, university, or private career school to students who are regularly enrolled at such institutions are exempt. For purposes of this subdivision, "instructional materials" means materials required to be used directly in the completion of the course of study, including, but not limited to, interactive CDs, tapes, digital audio works, digital audiovisual works, and computer software.</p>
          <p>Instructional materials do not include general reference works or other items incidental to the instructional process such as pens, pencils, paper, folders, or computers. For purposes of this subdivision, "school" and "private career school" have the meanings given in subdivision 13.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote"> Computers prescribed for use by school.</span></h2>
          <p> Computers and related computer software sold by a school, college, university, or private career school to students who are enrolled at the institutions are exempt if:</p>
          <p>(1) the use of the computer, or of a substantially similar model of computer, and the related computer software is prescribed by the institution in conjunction with a course of study; and</p>
          <p>(2) each student of the institution, or of a unit of the institution in which the student is enrolled, is required by the institution to have such a computer and related software as a condition of enrollment.</p>
          <p>For the purposes of this subdivision, "school" and "private career school" have the meanings given in subdivision 13.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Residential heating fuels.</span></h2>
          <p>Residential heating fuels are exempt as follows:</p>
          <p>(1) all fuel oil, coal, wood, steam, hot water, propane gas, and L.P. gas sold to residential customers for residential use;</p>
          <p>(2) for the billing months of November, December, January, February, March, and April, natural gas sold for residential use to customers who are metered and billed as residential users and who use natural gas for their primary source of residential heat; and</p>
          <p>(3) for the billing months of November, December, January, February, March, and April, electricity sold for residential use to customers who are metered and billed as residential users and who use electricity for their primary source of residential heat.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Residential water services.</span></h2>
          <p>Water services for residential use are exempt regardless of how the services are billed.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">Feminine hygiene products.</span></h2>
          <p>Sanitary napkins, tampons, or similar items used for feminine hygiene are exempt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">Used motor oils.</span></h2>
          <p>Used motor oils are exempt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.19">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.<span class="headnote">Cross-country ski passes.</span></h2>
          <p>Cross-country ski passes issued under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/85.40">85.40</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/85.43">85.43</a> are exempt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.20">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.<span class="headnote">Manufactured homes.</span></h2>
          <p>Manufactured homes, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/327.31#stat.327.31.6">327.31, subdivision 6</a>, to be used by the purchaser for residential purposes are exempt, unless the sale is the first retail sale of the manufactured home in this state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.21">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 21.<span class="headnote">De minimis exemption.</span></h2>
          <p>A purchase subject to use tax under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.63">297A.63</a> is exempt if (1) the purchase is made by an individual for personal use, and (2) the total purchases that are subject to the use tax do not exceed $770 in the calendar year. For purposes of this subdivision, "personal use" includes purchases for gifts. If an individual makes purchases subject to use tax of more than $770 in the calendar year, the individual must pay the use tax on the entire amount. This exemption does not apply to purchases made from retailers who are required or registered to collect taxes under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.22">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 22.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/154/#laws.12.41.0">2008 c 154 art 12 s 41</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.23">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 23.<span class="headnote">Occasional sales.</span></h2>
          <p>Isolated and occasional sales in Minnesota not made in the normal course of business of selling that kind of property or service are exempt. The storage, use, or consumption of property or services acquired as a result of such a sale is exempt. This exemption does not apply to sales of tangible personal property primarily used in a trade or business.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.24">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 24.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/88/#laws.8.5.0">2009 c 88 art 8 s 5</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.25">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 25.<span class="headnote">Maintenance of cemetery grounds.</span></h2>
          <p>Lawn care and related services used in the maintenance of cemetery grounds are exempt. For purposes of this subdivision, "lawn care and related services" means the services listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.61#stat.297A.61.3">297A.61, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (g), clause (6), item (vi), and "cemetery" means a cemetery for human burial.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.26">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 26.<span class="headnote">Trade allowance.</span></h2>
          <p>The amount allowed as a credit against the sales price for tangible personal property taken in trade for resale is exempt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.27">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 27.<span class="headnote">Sewing materials.</span></h2>
          <p>Sewing materials are exempt. For purposes of this subdivision "sewing materials" mean fabric, thread, zippers, interfacing, buttons, trim, and other items that are usually directly incorporated into the construction of clothing, as defined in subdivision 8, regardless of whether it is actually used for making clothing. It does not include batting, foam, or fabric specifically manufactured for arts and craft projects, or other materials for craft projects.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.28">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 28.<span class="headnote">Ambulance supplies, parts, and equipment.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The following sales to or use by an ambulance service licensed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144E.10">144E.10</a> are exempt:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) supplies and equipment used to provide medical care; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) repair and replacement parts for ambulances and vehicles equipped and specifically intended for emergency response.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.29">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 29.<span class="headnote">Solar energy products.</span></h2>
          <p>A solar energy system, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/216C.06#stat.216C.06.17">216C.06, subdivision 17</a>, is exempt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.30">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 30.<span class="headnote">Motor vehicles.</span></h2>
          <p>Motor vehicles taxable under the provisions of chapter 297B are exempt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.31">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 31.<span class="headnote">Service loaner vehicle covered by warranty.</span></h2>
          <p>The loan of a vehicle by a motor vehicle dealer to a customer as a replacement for a vehicle being serviced or repaired is exempt if the vehicle is loaned pursuant to a warranty included in the original purchase price of the vehicle being serviced or repaired.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.32">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 32.<span class="headnote">Cigarettes.</span></h2>
          <p>Cigarettes upon which a tax has been imposed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297F.25">297F.25</a> are exempt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.33">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 33.<span class="headnote">Presentations accessed as digital audio and audiovisual works.</span></h2>
          <p>The charge for a live or prerecorded presentation, such as a lecture, seminar, workshop, or course, where participants access the presentation as a digital audio work or digital audiovisual work, and are connected to the presentation via the Internet, telecommunications equipment, or other device that transfers the presentation electronically, is exempt if:</p>
          <p>(1) participants and the presenter, during the time that participants access the presentation, are able to give, receive, and discuss the presentation with each other, although the amount of interaction and when in the presentation the interaction occurs may be limited by the presenter; and</p>
          <p>(2) for those presentations where participants are given the option to attend the same presentation in person:</p>
          <p>(i) any limitations on the amount of interaction and when it occurs during the presentation are the same for those participants accessing the presentation electronically as those attending in person; and</p>
          <p>(ii) the admission to the in-person presentation is not subject to tax under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.34">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 34.<span class="headnote">Precious metal bullion.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Precious metal bullion is exempt. For purposes of this subdivision, "precious metal bullion" means bars or rounds that consist of 99.9 percent or more by weight of either gold, silver, platinum, or palladium and are marked with weight, purity, and content.</p>
          <p>(b) The exemption under this subdivision does not apply to sales and purchases of jewelry, works of art, or scrap metal.</p>
          <p>(c) The intent of this subdivision is to eliminate the difference in tax treatment between the sale of precious metal bullion and the sale of stock, bullion ETFs, bonds, and other investment instruments.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.35">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 35.<span class="headnote">Suite licenses.</span></h2>
          <p>The sale of the privilege of admission under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.61">297A.61</a>, subdivision 3, paragraph (g), clause (1), to a place of amusement or athletic event does not include consideration paid for a license to use a private suite, private skybox, or private box seat, and the sale of the license is exempt provided that: (1) the lessee may use the private suite, private skybox, or private box seat by mutual arrangement with the lessor on days when there is no amusement or athletic event; and (2) the sales price for the privilege of admission is equal to or greater than the highest priced general admission ticket for the closest seat not in the private suite, private skybox, or private box seat.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.36">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 36.<span class="headnote">Stadium builder's licenses.</span></h2>
          <p>The sale of the privilege of admission under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.61#stat.297A.61.3">297A.61, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (g), clause (1), does not include consideration paid for a stadium builder's license authorized under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/473J.15#stat.473J.15.14">473J.15, subdivision 14</a>, and the sale of the license is exempt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.37">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 37.<span class="headnote">Certain herbicides.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Purchases of herbicides authorized for use pursuant to an invasive aquatic plant management permit as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/103G.615">103G.615</a> are exempt if purchased by:</p>
          <p>(1) a lakeshore property owner;</p>
          <p>(2) an association of lakeshore property owners organized under chapter 317A; or</p>
          <p>(3) a contractor hired by a lakeshore owner or association to provide invasive aquatic plant management under the permit.</p>
          <p>(b) For purposes of this subdivision, "herbicides" means a substance or mixture of substances intended for use as a plant regulator, defoliant, or desiccant that are:</p>
          <p>(1) labeled for use in water;</p>
          <p>(2) registered for use in this state by the Department of Agriculture under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/18B.26">18B.26</a>; and</p>
          <p>(3) listed as one of the herbicides proposed for use on the invasive aquatic plant management permit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.38">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 38.<span class="headnote">Season ticket purchasing rights to collegiate events.</span></h2>
          <p>The sale of a right to purchase the privilege of admission to a college or university athletic event in a preferred viewing location for a season of a particular athletic event is exempt provided that:</p>
          <p>(1) the consideration paid for the right to purchase is used entirely to support student scholarships, wellness, and academic costs; and</p>
          <p>(2) the admission price is equal to or greater than the highest priced general admission ticket for the closest seat not in the preferred viewing location.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.39">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 39.<span class="headnote">Reservation sales of taxable cannabis products.</span></h2>
          <p>The sale of a taxable cannabis product, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/295.81#stat.295.81.1">295.81, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (r), that is made on Tribally regulated land as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/3.9228#stat.3.9228.1">3.9228, subdivision 1</a>, by a cannabis business licensed by a Minnesota Tribal government, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/3.9228">3.9228</a>, subdivision 1, paragraph (f), is exempt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.67.40">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 40.<span class="headnote">Firearm storage units.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Secure firearm storage units are exempt. For the purposes of this subdivision:</p>
          <p>(1) "secure firearm storage unit" means a container that is fully enclosed and locked by a padlock, keylock, combination lock, or similar locking device, and is either specifically designed for the safe storage of firearms or sold for that purpose by a federally licensed firearms dealer; and</p>
          <p>(2) "firearm" has the meaning provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.015#stat.97A.015.19">97A.015, subdivision 19</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) The seller of a secure firearm storage unit must neither collect, nor transmit to any private or public entity, any personal data of or information about a purchaser resulting from a sale eligible for the exemption under this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.67.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.11.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 11</a>,44 subd 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/490/#laws.8.13.0">2000 c 490 art 8 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.7.51.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 7 s 51</a>; art 12 s 36-44,93; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/377/#laws.3.7.0">2002 c 377 art 3 s 7</a>-10; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/400/#laws.0.8.0">2002 c 400 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/127/#laws.1.21.0">2003 c 127 art 1 s 21</a>,22; art 6 s 9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/56/#laws.0.1.0">2005 c 56 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/151/#laws.7.11.0">2005 c 151 art 7 s 11</a>-13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/3/#laws.5.5.0">1Sp2005 c 3 art 5 s 5</a>-7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/259/#laws.6.11.0">2006 c 259 art 6 s 11</a>-15; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/154/#laws.12.26.0">2008 c 154 art 12 s 26</a>-28; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/366/#laws.7.1.0">2008 c 366 art 7 s 1</a>; art 13 s 5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/143/#laws.8.22.0">2013 c 143 art 8 s 22</a>-24; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/308/#laws.3.6.0">2014 c 308 art 3 s 6</a>,7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/69/#laws.2.46.0">2015 c 69 art 2 s 46</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/1/#laws.3.13.0">1Sp2017 c 1 art 3 s 13</a>-19; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/6/#laws.3.7.0">1Sp2019 c 6 art 3 s 7</a>; c 9 art 1 s 42; art 24 s 20,21; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/14/#laws.4.4.0">1Sp2021 c 14 art 4 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/63/#laws.2.12.0">2023 c 63 art 2 s 12</a>-14; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/64/#laws.5.3.0">2023 c 64 art 5 s 3</a>-5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/121/#laws.2.35.0">2024 c 121 art 2 s 35</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.68">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.68 BUSINESS EXEMPTIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope.</span></h2>
          <p>The gross receipts from the sale of, and storage, distribution, use, or consumption of the items contained in this section are specifically exempted from the taxes imposed by this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Materials consumed in industrial production.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Materials stored, used, or consumed in industrial production of tangible personal property intended to be sold ultimately at retail, are exempt, whether or not the item so used becomes an ingredient or constituent part of the property produced. Materials that qualify for this exemption include, but are not limited to, the following:</p>
          <p>(1) chemicals, including chemicals used for cleaning food processing machinery and equipment;</p>
          <p>(2) materials, including chemicals, fuels, and electricity purchased by persons engaged in industrial production to treat waste generated as a result of the production process;</p>
          <p>(3) fuels, electricity, gas, and steam used or consumed in the production process, except that electricity, gas, or steam used for space heating, cooling, or lighting is exempt if (i) it is in excess of the average climate control or lighting for the production area, and (ii) it is necessary to produce that particular product;</p>
          <p>(4) petroleum products and lubricants;</p>
          <p>(5) packaging materials, including returnable containers used in packaging food and beverage products;</p>
          <p>(6) accessory tools, equipment, and other items that are separate detachable units with an ordinary useful life of less than 12 months used in producing a direct effect upon the product; and</p>
          <p>(7) the following materials, tools, and equipment used in metal-casting: crucibles, thermocouple protection sheaths and tubes, stalk tubes, refractory materials, molten metal filters and filter boxes, degassing lances, and base blocks.</p>
          <p>(b) This exemption does not include:</p>
          <p>(1) machinery, equipment, implements, tools, accessories, appliances, contrivances and furniture and fixtures, except those listed in paragraph (a), clause (6); and</p>
          <p>(2) petroleum and special fuels used in producing or generating power for propelling ready-mixed concrete trucks on the public highways of this state.</p>
          <p>(c) Industrial production includes, but is not limited to, research, development, design or production of any tangible personal property, manufacturing, processing (other than by restaurants and consumers) of agricultural products (whether vegetable or animal), commercial fishing, refining, smelting, reducing, brewing, distilling, printing, mining, quarrying, lumbering, generating electricity, the production of road building materials, and the research, development, design, or production of computer software. Industrial production does not include painting, cleaning, repairing or similar processing of property except as part of the original manufacturing process.</p>
          <p>(d) Industrial production does not include:</p>
          <p>(1) the furnishing of services listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.61#stat.297A.61.3">297A.61, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (g), clause (6), items (i) to (vi) and (viii); or</p>
          <p>(2) the transportation, transmission, or distribution of petroleum, liquefied gas, natural gas, water, or steam, in, by, or through pipes, lines, tanks, mains, or other means of transporting those products. For purposes of this paragraph, "transportation, transmission, or distribution" does not include blending of petroleum or biodiesel fuel as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/239.77">239.77</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Materials used in providing certain taxable services.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Materials stored, used, or consumed in providing a taxable service listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.61#stat.297A.61.3">297A.61, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (g), clause (6), intended to be sold ultimately at retail are exempt.</p>
          <p>(b) This exemption includes, but is not limited to:</p>
          <p>(1) chemicals, lubricants, packaging materials, seeds, trees, fertilizers, and herbicides, if these items are used or consumed in providing the taxable service;</p>
          <p>(2) chemicals used to treat waste generated as a result of providing the taxable service;</p>
          <p>(3) accessory tools, equipment, and other items that are separate detachable units used in providing the service and that have an ordinary useful life of less than 12 months; and</p>
          <p>(4) fuel, electricity, gas, and steam used or consumed in the production process, except that electricity, gas, or steam used for space heating, cooling, or lighting is exempt if (i) it is in excess of average climate control or lighting, and (ii) it is necessary to produce that particular service.</p>
          <p>(c) This exemption does not include machinery, equipment, implements, tools, accessories, appliances, contrivances, furniture, and fixtures used in providing the taxable service.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.3a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Coin-operated entertainment and amusement devices.</span></h2>
          <p>Coin-operated entertainment and amusement devices including, but not limited to, fortune-telling machines, cranes, foosball and pool tables, video and pinball games, batting cages, rides, photo or video booths, and jukeboxes are exempt when purchased by retailers selling admission to places of amusement and making available amusement devices as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.61#stat.297A.61.3">297A.61, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (g), clause (1). Coin-operated entertainment and amusement devices do not include vending machines, lottery devices, or gaming devices as described in chapters 297E and 349.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Taconite, other ores, metals, or minerals; production materials.</span></h2>
          <p>Mill liners, grinding rods, and grinding balls that are substantially consumed in the production of taconite or other ores, metals, or minerals are exempt when sold to or stored, used, or consumed by persons taxed under the in-lieu or gross proceeds provisions of chapter 298.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Capital equipment.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Capital equipment is exempt.</p>
          <p>"Capital equipment" means machinery and equipment purchased or leased, and used in this state by the purchaser or lessee primarily for manufacturing, fabricating, mining, or refining tangible personal property to be sold ultimately at retail if the machinery and equipment are essential to the integrated production process of manufacturing, fabricating, mining, or refining. Capital equipment also includes machinery and equipment used primarily to electronically transmit results retrieved by a customer of an online computerized data retrieval system.</p>
          <p>(b) Capital equipment includes, but is not limited to:</p>
          <p>(1) machinery and equipment used to operate, control, or regulate the production equipment;</p>
          <p>(2) machinery and equipment used for research and development, design, quality control, and testing activities;</p>
          <p>(3) environmental control devices that are used to maintain conditions such as temperature, humidity, light, or air pressure when those conditions are essential to and are part of the production process;</p>
          <p>(4) materials and supplies used to construct and install machinery or equipment;</p>
          <p>(5) repair and replacement parts, including accessories, whether purchased as spare parts, repair parts, or as upgrades or modifications to machinery or equipment;</p>
          <p>(6) materials used for foundations that support machinery or equipment;</p>
          <p>(7) materials used to construct and install special purpose buildings used in the production process;</p>
          <p>(8) ready-mixed concrete equipment in which the ready-mixed concrete is mixed as part of the delivery process regardless if mounted on a chassis, repair parts for ready-mixed concrete trucks, and leases of ready-mixed concrete trucks; and</p>
          <p>(9) machinery or equipment used for research, development, design, or production of computer software.</p>
          <p>(c) Capital equipment does not include the following:</p>
          <p>(1) motor vehicles taxed under chapter 297B;</p>
          <p>(2) machinery or equipment used to receive or store raw materials;</p>
          <p>(3) building materials, except for materials included in paragraph (b), clauses (6) and (7);</p>
          <p>(4) machinery or equipment used for nonproduction purposes, including, but not limited to, the following: plant security, fire prevention, first aid, and hospital stations; support operations or administration; pollution control; and plant cleaning, disposal of scrap and waste, plant communications, space heating, cooling, lighting, or safety;</p>
          <p>(5) farm machinery and aquaculture production equipment as defined by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.61">297A.61</a>, subdivisions 12 and 13;</p>
          <p>(6) machinery or equipment purchased and installed by a contractor as part of an improvement to real property;</p>
          <p>(7) machinery and equipment used by restaurants in the furnishing, preparing, or serving of prepared foods as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.61#stat.297A.61.31">297A.61, subdivision 31</a>;</p>
          <p>(8) machinery and equipment used to furnish the services listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.61#stat.297A.61.3">297A.61, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (g), clause (6), items (i) to (vi) and (viii);</p>
          <p>(9) machinery or equipment used in the transportation, transmission, or distribution of petroleum, liquefied gas, natural gas, water, or steam, in, by, or through pipes, lines, tanks, mains, or other means of transporting those products. This clause does not apply to machinery or equipment used to blend petroleum or biodiesel fuel as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/239.77">239.77</a>; or</p>
          <p>(10) any other item that is not essential to the integrated process of manufacturing, fabricating, mining, or refining.</p>
          <p>(d) For purposes of this subdivision:</p>
          <p>(1) "Equipment" means independent devices or tools separate from machinery but essential to an integrated production process, including computers and computer software, used in operating, controlling, or regulating machinery and equipment; and any subunit or assembly comprising a component of any machinery or accessory or attachment parts of machinery, such as tools, dies, jigs, patterns, and molds.</p>
          <p>(2) "Fabricating" means to make, build, create, produce, or assemble components or property to work in a new or different manner.</p>
          <p>(3) "Integrated production process" means a process or series of operations through which tangible personal property is manufactured, fabricated, mined, or refined. For purposes of this clause, (i) manufacturing begins with the removal of raw materials from inventory and ends when the last process prior to loading for shipment has been completed; (ii) fabricating begins with the removal from storage or inventory of the property to be assembled, processed, altered, or modified and ends with the creation or production of the new or changed product; (iii) mining begins with the removal of overburden from the site of the ores, minerals, stone, peat deposit, or surface materials and ends when the last process before stockpiling is completed; and (iv) refining begins with the removal from inventory or storage of a natural resource and ends with the conversion of the item to its completed form.</p>
          <p>(4) "Machinery" means mechanical, electronic, or electrical devices, including computers and computer software, that are purchased or constructed to be used for the activities set forth in paragraph (a), beginning with the removal of raw materials from inventory through completion of the product, including packaging of the product.</p>
          <p>(5) "Machinery and equipment used for pollution control" means machinery and equipment used solely to eliminate, prevent, or reduce pollution resulting from an activity described in paragraph (a).</p>
          <p>(6) "Manufacturing" means an operation or series of operations where raw materials are changed in form, composition, or condition by machinery and equipment and which results in the production of a new article of tangible personal property. For purposes of this subdivision, "manufacturing" includes the generation of electricity or steam to be sold at retail.</p>
          <p>(7) "Mining" means the extraction of minerals, ores, stone, or peat.</p>
          <p>(8) "Online data retrieval system" means a system whose cumulation of information is equally available and accessible to all its customers.</p>
          <p>(9) "Primarily" means machinery and equipment used 50 percent or more of the time in an activity described in paragraph (a).</p>
          <p>(10) "Refining" means the process of converting a natural resource to an intermediate or finished product, including the treatment of water to be sold at retail.</p>
          <p>(11) This subdivision does not apply to telecommunications equipment as provided in subdivision 35a, and does not apply to wire, cable, or poles for telecommunications services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Special tooling.</span></h2>
          <p>Special tooling is exempt. "Special tooling" means tools, dies, jigs, patterns, gauges, and other special tools that have value and use only for the buyer and for the use for which they are made. An item has value and use only to the buyer if the item is not standard enough to be stocked or ordered from a catalog or other sales literature, but must be produced in accordance with special requirements peculiar to the buyer and not common to someone else whose conditions for possible use of the material are reasonably similar to the buyer's.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Air cooling equipment.</span></h2>
          <p>Equipment used for air cooling is exempt, if the equipment is purchased for conversion or replacement of an existing groundwater-based once-through cooling system as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/103G.271#stat.103G.271.5">103G.271, subdivision 5</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Pollution control equipment; steel reprocessing.</span></h2>
          <p>Pollution control equipment purchased by a steel reprocessing firm is exempt if the equipment is necessary to meet state or federal emission standards. For purposes of this subdivision:</p>
          <p>(1) "pollution control equipment" means equipment used for the purpose of eliminating, preventing, or reducing air, land, or water pollution during or as a result of the manufacturing process; and</p>
          <p>(2) "steel reprocessing firm" means a firm whose primary business is the recovery of steel from automobiles, appliances, and other steel products and the rerefining of this recovered metal into new steel products.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Super Bowl admissions and related events.</span></h2>
          <p> The granting of the privilege of admission to a world championship football game sponsored by the National Football League and to related events sponsored by the National Football League is exempt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Publications; publication materials.</span></h2>
          <p>Tangible personal property that is used or consumed in producing any publication regularly issued at average intervals not exceeding three months is exempt, and any such publication is exempt. "Publication" includes, but is not limited to, a qualified newspaper as defined by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/331A.02">331A.02</a>, together with any supplements or enclosures. "Publication" does not include magazines and periodicals sold over the counter. Tangible personal property that is used or consumed in producing a publication does not include machinery, equipment, implements, tools, accessories, appliances, contrivances, furniture, and fixtures used in the publication, or fuel, electricity, gas, or steam used for space heating or lighting.</p>
          <p>Advertising contained in a publication is a nontaxable service and is exempt. Persons who publish or sell newspapers are engaging in a nontaxable service with respect to gross receipts realized from such news-gathering or news-publishing activities, including the sale of advertising.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Advertising materials.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Materials designed to advertise and promote the sale of merchandise or services are exempt if these materials are mailed or transferred to a person outside the state for use solely outside the state. Mailing and reply envelopes and cards and other shipping materials including, but not limited to, boxes, labels, containers, and banding, used exclusively in connection with these advertising and promotional materials are included in this exemption. The exemption applies regardless of where the mailing occurs. The storage of these materials in the state for the purpose of subsequently shipping or otherwise transferring the material out of state is also exempt if the other conditions in this subdivision are met. For purposes of this subdivision, materials that have a primary purpose other than advertising, such as fulfilling a legal obligation or furnishing nonadvertising information, are not materials designed to advertise and promote the sale of merchandise or services even if they do include advertising content.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Wind energy conversion systems.</span></h2>
          <p>Wind energy conversion systems, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/216C.06#stat.216C.06.19">216C.06, subdivision 19</a>, that are used as an electric power source are exempt, and the materials used to manufacture, install, construct, repair, or replace them are exempt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Outstate transport or delivery.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Tangible personal property is exempt if all of the following conditions are met:</p>
          <p>(1) the property, without intermediate use, is shipped or transported outside Minnesota by the purchaser or is stored, processed, fabricated or manufactured into, attached to or incorporated into other tangible personal property that is transported or shipped outside Minnesota; and</p>
          <p>(2) the property is used in a trade or business outside Minnesota after being shipped or transported outside of Minnesota, and is not returned to Minnesota, except in the course of interstate commerce; and</p>
          <p>(3) the property is either (i) not subject to tax in the state or country to which it is transported for storage or use, or (ii) to be used in other states or countries as part of a maintenance contract.</p>
          <p>(b) For purposes of this subdivision, storage or processing, fabricating, manufacturing, attaching to, or incorporating into other property is not intermediate use.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Property in transit.</span></h2>
          <p>Tangible personal property is exempt if all of the following conditions are met:</p>
          <p>(1) it is shipped or brought into Minnesota by a for-hire carrier;</p>
          <p>(2) without use, it is kept in a public warehouse;</p>
          <p>(3) it is kept for the purpose of being later transported outside Minnesota; and</p>
          <p>(4) after storage, it is used solely outside Minnesota, except in the course of interstate commerce.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/259/#laws.6.32.0">2006 c 259 art 6 s 32</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Packing materials.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Packing materials used to pack and ship household goods and that are provided to and remain with the customer of a for-hire carrier are exempt if the ultimate destination of the goods is outside Minnesota and if the packing materials are not later returned to a point within Minnesota, except in the course of interstate commerce. This exemption does not apply to tools, equipment, pads, or accessories owned or leased by the for-hire carrier.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">Ships used in interstate commerce; other vessels.</span></h2>
          <p>Repair, replacement, and rebuilding parts and materials, and lubricants, for the following are exempt:</p>
          <p>(1) ships or vessels used or to be used principally in interstate or foreign commerce; and</p>
          <p>(2) vessels with a gross registered tonnage of at least 3,000 tons.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/259/#laws.6.32.0">2006 c 259 art 6 s 32</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.19">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.<span class="headnote">Petroleum products.</span></h2>
          <p>The following petroleum products are exempt:</p>
          <p>(1) products upon which a tax has been imposed and paid under chapter 296A, and for which no refund has been or will be allowed because the buyer used the fuel for nonhighway use;</p>
          <p>(2) products that are used in the improvement of agricultural land by constructing, maintaining, and repairing drainage ditches, tile drainage systems, grass waterways, water impoundment, and other erosion control structures;</p>
          <p>(3) products purchased by a transit system receiving financial assistance under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/174.24">174.24</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0625#stat.256B.0625.17">256B.0625, subdivision 17</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/473.384">473.384</a>;</p>
          <p>(4) products purchased by an ambulance service licensed under chapter 144E;</p>
          <p>(5) products used in a passenger snowmobile, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/296A.01#stat.296A.01.39">296A.01, subdivision 39</a>, for off-highway business use as part of the operations of a resort as provided under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/296A.16#stat.296A.16.2">296A.16, subdivision 2</a>, clause (2);</p>
          <p>(6) products purchased by a state or a political subdivision of a state for use in motor vehicles exempt from registration under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/168.012#stat.168.012.1">168.012, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b);</p>
          <p>(7) products purchased by providers of transportation to recipients of medical assistance home and community-based services waivers enrolled in day programs, including adult day care, family adult day care, day treatment and habilitation, prevocational services, and structured day services;</p>
          <p>(8) products used in a motor vehicle used exclusively as a mobile medical unit for the provision of medical or dental services by a federally qualified health center, as defined under title 19 of the federal Social Security Act, as amended by section 4161 of the Omnibus Budget Reconciliation Act of 1990; or</p>
          <p>(9) special fuel used for one of the following purposes:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) to power a refrigeration unit mounted on a licensed motor vehicle, provided that the unit has an engine separate from the one used to propel the vehicle and the fuel is used exclusively for the unit;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) to power an unlicensed motor vehicle that is used solely or primarily to move semitrailers within a cargo yard, warehouse facility, or intermodal facility; or</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) to operate a power take-off unit or auxiliary engine in or on a licensed motor vehicle, whether or not the unit or engine is fueled from the same or a different fuel tank as that from which the motor vehicle is fueled.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.20">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.<span class="headnote">Natural gas in vehicles.</span></h2>
          <p>Natural gas to be used as a fuel in vehicles propelled by natural gas is exempt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.21">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 21.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.12.95.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 12 s 95</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.22">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 22.<span class="headnote">Copies of court reporter documents.</span></h2>
          <p>Transcripts or copies of transcripts of verbatim testimony are exempt if produced and sold by court reporters or other transcribers of legal proceedings to individuals or entities that are parties to or representatives of parties to the proceeding to which the transcript relates.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.23">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 23.<span class="headnote">Automatic fire-safety sprinkler systems.</span></h2>
          <p>Automatic fire-safety sprinkler systems described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.11#stat.273.11.6a">273.11, subdivision 6a</a>, are exempt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.24">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 24.<span class="headnote">Waste processing equipment.</span></h2>
          <p>Equipment used for processing solid or hazardous waste at a resource recovery facility, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.03#stat.115A.03.28">115A.03, subdivision 28</a>, is exempt, including pollution control equipment at a resource recovery facility that burns refuse-derived fuel or mixed municipal solid waste as its primary fuel. An electric generation facility that processes and utilizes waste tires as its primary fuel is a resource recovery facility for the purposes of this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.25">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 25.<span class="headnote">Sale of property used in a trade or business.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The sale of tangible personal property primarily used in a trade or business is exempt if the sale is not made in the normal course of business of selling that kind of property and if one of the following conditions is satisfied:</p>
          <p>(1) the sale occurs in a transaction subject to or described in section 118, 331, 332, 336, 337, 338, 351, 355, 368, 721, 731, 1031, or 1033 of the Internal Revenue Code, as amended through December 16, 2016;</p>
          <p>(2) the sale is between members of a controlled group as defined in section 1563(a) of the Internal Revenue Code;</p>
          <p>(3) the sale is between a sole member of a disregarded limited liability company and the disregarded limited liability company;</p>
          <p>(4) the sale is a sale of farm machinery;</p>
          <p>(5) the sale is a farm auction sale;</p>
          <p>(6) the sale is a sale of substantially all of the assets of a trade or business; or</p>
          <p>(7) the total amount of gross receipts from the sale of trade or business property made during the calendar month of the sale and the preceding 11 calendar months does not exceed $1,000.</p>
          <p>The use, storage, distribution, or consumption of tangible personal property acquired as a result of a sale exempt under this subdivision is also exempt.</p>
          <p>(b) For purposes of this subdivision, the following terms have the meanings given.</p>
          <p>(1) "Disregarded limited liability company" means a limited liability company that is disregarded as an entity separate from its owner under the Internal Revenue Code.</p>
          <p>(2) A "farm auction" is a public auction conducted by a licensed auctioneer if substantially all of the property sold consists of property used in the trade or business of farming and property not used primarily in a trade or business.</p>
          <p>(3) "Trade or business" includes the assets of a separate division, branch, or identifiable segment of a trade or business if, before the sale, the income and expenses attributable to the separate division, branch, or identifiable segment could be separately ascertained from the books of account or record (the lease or rental of an identifiable segment does not qualify for the exemption).</p>
          <p>(4) A "sale of substantially all of the assets of a trade or business" must occur as a single transaction or a series of related transactions within the 12-month period beginning on the date of the first sale of assets intended to qualify for the exemption provided in paragraph (a), clause (5).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.26">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 26.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/377/#laws.3.26.0">2002 c 377 art 3 s 26</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.27">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 27.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.67">297A.67</a>, subd 30]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.28">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 28.<span class="headnote">Medical supplies.</span></h2>
          <p>Medical supplies purchased by a licensed health care facility or licensed health care professional to provide medical treatment to residents or patients are exempt. The exemption does not apply to durable medical equipment or components of durable medical equipment, laboratory supplies, radiological supplies, and other items used in providing medical services. For purposes of this subdivision, "medical supplies" means adhesive and nonadhesive bandages, gauze pads and strips, cotton applicators, antiseptics, eye solution, and other similar supplies used directly on the resident or patient in providing medical services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.29">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 29.<span class="headnote">Prizes.</span></h2>
          <p>Tangible personal property that will be given as prizes to players in games of skill or chance is exempt if the games are conducted at events such as community festivals, fairs, and carnivals and if the events last less than six days. This exemption does not apply to property awarded as prizes in connection with lawful gambling as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/349.12">349.12</a> or the State Lottery.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.30">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 30.<span class="headnote">Television commercials.</span></h2>
          <p>Tangible personal property primarily used or consumed in the preproduction, production, or postproduction of a television commercial is exempt. Any such commercial, regardless of the medium in which it is transferred, is exempt. "Preproduction" and "production" include, but are not limited to, all activities related to the preparation for shooting and the shooting of television commercials, including film processing. Equipment rented for the preproduction and production activities is exempt. "Postproduction" includes, but is not limited to, all activities related to the finishing and duplication of television commercials. This exemption does not apply to tangible personal property used primarily in administration, general management, or marketing. Machinery and equipment purchased for use in producing such commercials and fuel, electricity, gas, or steam used for space heating or lighting are not exempt under this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.31">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 31.<span class="headnote">Waste management containers and compactors.</span></h2>
          <p>Compactors and waste collection containers are exempt if they are purchased by a waste management service provider and are used in providing waste management services as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297H.01#stat.297H.01.12">297H.01, subdivision 12</a>. A waste management service provider that does not remit tax on customer charges or lease or rental payments for compactors and waste collection containers under chapter 297H is ineligible for this exemption.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.32">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 32.<span class="headnote">Events located outside Minnesota.</span></h2>
          <p>Tickets or admissions to places of amusement located outside Minnesota or to athletic events to be held outside Minnesota are exempt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.33">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 33.<span class="headnote">Patent, trademark, and copyright drawings and documents.</span></h2>
          <p>A drawing, diagram, or similar or related document or a copy of such a document is exempt if the document:</p>
          <p>(1) is produced and sold by a patent drafter; and</p>
          <p>(2) is for use in:</p>
          <p>(i) a patent, trademark, or copyright application to be filed with government agencies;</p>
          <p>(ii) an application to the federal Food and Drug Administration for approval of a medical device; or</p>
          <p>(iii) a judicial or quasi-judicial proceeding, including mediation and arbitration, relating to the validity of or legal rights under a patent, trademark, or copyright.</p>
          <p>For purposes of this subdivision, a "patent drafter" is a person who prepares illustrative documents required in the preparation of intellectual property applications.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.34">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 34.<span class="headnote">Machinery and equipment for ski areas.</span></h2>
          <p>Tangible personal property used or consumed primarily and directly for tramways at ski areas or in snowmaking and snow-grooming operations at ski hills, ski slopes, or ski trails, including machinery, equipment, fuel, electricity, and water additives used in the production and maintenance of machine-made snow, is exempt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.35">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 35.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/143/#laws.8.53.0">2013 c 143 art 8 s 53</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.35a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 35a.<span class="headnote">Telecommunications or pay television services machinery and equipment.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Telecommunications or pay television services machinery and equipment purchased or leased for use directly by a telecommunications or pay television services provider primarily in the provision of telecommunications or pay television services that are ultimately to be sold at retail are exempt, regardless of whether purchased by the owner, a contractor, or a subcontractor.</p>
          <p>(b) For purposes of this subdivision, "telecommunications or pay television machinery and equipment" includes, but is not limited to:</p>
          <p>(1) machinery, equipment, and fixtures utilized in receiving, initiating, amplifying, processing, transmitting, retransmitting, recording, switching, or monitoring telecommunications or pay television services, such as computers, transformers, amplifiers, routers, bridges, repeaters, multiplexers, and other items performing comparable functions;</p>
          <p>(2) machinery, equipment, and fixtures used in the transportation of telecommunications or pay television services, such as radio transmitters and receivers, satellite equipment, microwave equipment, fiber, conduit, and other transporting media, but not wire, cable, or poles;</p>
          <p>(3) ancillary machinery, equipment, and fixtures that regulate, control, protect, or enable the machinery in clauses (1) and (2) to accomplish its intended function, such as auxiliary power supply, test equipment, towers, heating, ventilating, and air conditioning equipment necessary to the operation of the telecommunications or pay television equipment; and software necessary to the operation of the telecommunications or pay television equipment; and</p>
          <p>(4) repair and replacement parts, including accessories, whether purchased as spare parts, repair parts, or as upgrades or modifications to qualified machinery or equipment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.36">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 36.<span class="headnote">Delivery or distribution charges; direct mail.</span></h2>
          <p>Charges for the delivery or distribution of direct mail are exempt if the charges are separately stated on an invoice or similar billing document given to the purchaser.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.37">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 37.<span class="headnote">Job opportunity building zones.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Purchases of tangible personal property or taxable services by a qualified business, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/469.310">469.310</a>, are exempt if the property or services are primarily used or consumed in a job opportunity building zone designated under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/469.314">469.314</a>. For purposes of this subdivision, an aerial camera package, including any camera, computer, and navigation device contained in the package, that is used in an aircraft that is operated under a Federal Aviation Administration Restricted Airworthiness Certificate according to Code of Federal Regulations, title 14, part 21, section 21.25(b)(3), relating to aerial surveying, and that is based, maintained, and dispatched from a job opportunity building zone, qualifies as primarily used or consumed in a job opportunity building zone if the imagery acquired from the aerial camera package is returned to the job opportunity building zone for processing. The exemption for an aerial camera package is limited as provided in this subdivision and the tax must be imposed and collected as if the rate under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.62#stat.297A.62.1">297A.62, subdivision 1</a>, applied and then refunded in the manner provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.75">297A.75</a>. The total amount of the aerial camera package exemption refunded for all taxpayers for all fiscal years is limited to $50,000 in taxes.</p>
          <p>(b) Purchase and use of construction materials and supplies used or consumed in, and equipment incorporated into, the construction of improvements to real property in a job opportunity building zone are exempt if the improvements after completion of construction are to be used in the conduct of a qualified business, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/469.310">469.310</a>. This exemption applies regardless of whether the purchases are made by the business or a contractor.</p>
          <p>(c) The exemptions under this subdivision apply to a local sales and use tax regardless of whether the local sales tax is imposed on the sales taxable as defined under this chapter.</p>
          <p>(d) This subdivision applies to sales, if the purchase was made and delivery received during the duration of the zone.</p>
          <p>(e) Notwithstanding the restriction in paragraph (a), which requires items purchased to be primarily used or consumed in the zone, purchases by a qualified business that is an electrical cooperative located in Meeker County of equipment and materials used for the generation, transmission, and distribution of electrical energy are exempt under this subdivision, except that:</p>
          <p>(1) the exemption for materials and equipment used or consumed outside the zone must not exceed $200,000 in taxes for all taxpayers for all fiscal years; and</p>
          <p>(2) no sales and use tax exemption is allowed for equipment purchased for resale.</p>
          <p class="bl">For purposes of this paragraph, the tax must be imposed and collected as if the rate under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.62#stat.297A.62.1">297A.62, subdivision 1</a>, applied and then refunded in the manner provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.75">297A.75</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.38">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 38.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/308/#laws.9.94.0">2014 c 308 art 9 s 94</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.39">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 39.<span class="headnote">Preexisting bids or contracts.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The sale of tangible personal property or services is exempt from tax or a tax rate increase for a period of six months from the effective date of the law change that results in the imposition of the tax or the tax rate increase under this chapter if:</p>
          <p>(1) the act imposing the tax or increasing the tax rate does not have transitional effective date language for existing construction contracts and construction bids; and</p>
          <p>(2) the requirements of paragraph (b) are met.</p>
          <p>(b) A sale is tax exempt under paragraph (a) if it meets the requirements of either clause (1) or (2):</p>
          <p>(1) For a construction contract:</p>
          <p>(i) the goods or services sold must be used for the performance of a bona fide written lump sum or fixed price construction contract;</p>
          <p>(ii) the contract must be entered into before the date the goods or services become subject to the sales tax or the tax rate was increased;</p>
          <p>(iii) the contract must not provide for allocation of future taxes; and</p>
          <p>(iv) for each qualifying contract the contractor must keep documentation of the contract on which an exemption is to be claimed.</p>
          <p>(2) For a construction bid:</p>
          <p>(i) the goods or services sold must be used pursuant to an obligation of a bid or bids;</p>
          <p>(ii) the bid or bids must be submitted and accepted before the date the goods or services became subject to the sales tax or the tax rate was increased;</p>
          <p>(iii) the bid or bids must not be able to be withdrawn, modified, or changed without forfeiting a bond; and</p>
          <p>(iv) for each qualifying bid, the contractor must keep documentation of the bid on which an exemption is to be claimed.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.40">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 40.<span class="headnote">Land clearing.</span></h2>
          <p>Tree, bush, shrub, and stump removal are exempt when sold to contractors or subcontractors as part of a land clearing contract. For purposes of this subdivision, "land clearing contract" means a contract for the removal of trees, bushes, and shrubs, including the removal of roots and stumps, to develop a site. This exemption does not apply to land clearing of a portion of a site to allow for remodeling, improvement, or expansion of an existing structure.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.41">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 41.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/294/#laws.2.43.0">2012 c 294 art 2 s 43</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.42">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 42.<span class="headnote">Qualified data centers.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Purchases of enterprise information technology equipment and computer software for use in a qualified data center, or a qualified refurbished data center, are exempt, except that computer software maintenance agreements are exempt for purchases made after June 30, 2013. The tax on purchases exempt under this paragraph must be imposed and collected as if the rate under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.62#stat.297A.62.1">297A.62, subdivision 1</a>, applied, and then refunded after June 30, 2013, in the manner provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.75">297A.75</a>. This exemption includes enterprise information technology equipment and computer software purchased to replace or upgrade enterprise information technology equipment and computer software in a qualified data center, or a qualified refurbished data center.</p>
          <p>(b) Electricity used or consumed in the operation of a qualified data center or qualified refurbished data center is exempt.</p>
          <p>(c) For purposes of this subdivision, "qualified data center" means a facility in Minnesota:</p>
          <p>(1) that is comprised of one or more buildings that consist in the aggregate of at least 25,000 square feet, and that are located on a single parcel or on contiguous parcels, where the total cost of construction or refurbishment, investment in enterprise information technology equipment, and computer software is at least $30,000,000 within a 48-month period. The 48-month period begins no sooner than July 1, 2012, except that costs for computer software maintenance agreements purchased before July 1, 2013, are not included in determining if the $30,000,000 threshold has been met;</p>
          <p>(2) that is constructed or substantially refurbished after June 30, 2012, where "substantially refurbished" means that at least 25,000 square feet have been rebuilt or modified, including:</p>
          <p>(i) installation of enterprise information technology equipment; environmental control, computer software, and energy efficiency improvements; and</p>
          <p>(ii) building improvements; and</p>
          <p>(3) that is used to house enterprise information technology equipment, where the facility has the following characteristics:</p>
          <p>(i) uninterruptible power supplies, generator backup power, or both;</p>
          <p>(ii) sophisticated fire suppression and prevention systems; and</p>
          <p>(iii) enhanced security. A facility will be considered to have enhanced security if it has restricted access to the facility to selected personnel; permanent security guards; video camera surveillance; an electronic system requiring pass codes, keycards, or biometric scans, such as hand scans and retinal or fingerprint recognition; or similar security features.</p>
          <p>In determining whether the facility has the required square footage, the square footage of the following spaces shall be included if the spaces support the operation of enterprise information technology equipment: office space, meeting space, and mechanical and other support facilities. For purposes of this subdivision, "computer software" includes, but is not limited to, software utilized or loaded at a qualified data center or qualified refurbished data center, including maintenance, licensing, and software customization.</p>
          <p>(d) For purposes of this subdivision, a "qualified refurbished data center" means an existing facility that qualifies as a data center under paragraph (c), clauses (2) and (3), but that is comprised of one or more buildings that consist in the aggregate of at least 25,000 square feet, and that are located on a single parcel or contiguous parcels, where the total cost of construction or refurbishment, investment in enterprise information technology equipment, and computer software is at least $50,000,000 within a 24-month period.</p>
          <p>(e) For purposes of this subdivision, "enterprise information technology equipment" means computers and equipment supporting computing, networking, or data storage, including servers and routers. It includes, but is not limited to: cooling systems, cooling towers, and other temperature control infrastructure; power infrastructure for transformation, distribution, or management of electricity used for the maintenance and operation of a qualified data center or qualified refurbished data center, including but not limited to exterior dedicated business-owned substations, backup power generation systems, battery systems, and related infrastructure; and racking systems, cabling, and trays, which are necessary for the maintenance and operation of the qualified data center or qualified refurbished data center.</p>
          <p>(f) A qualified data center or qualified refurbished data center may claim the exemptions in this subdivision for purchases made either within 20 years of the date of its first purchase qualifying for the exemption under paragraph (a), or by June 30, 2042, whichever is earlier.</p>
          <p>(g) The purpose of this exemption is to create jobs in the construction and data center industries.</p>
          <p>(h) This subdivision is effective for sales and purchases made before July 1, 2042.</p>
          <p>(i) The commissioner of employment and economic development must certify to the commissioner of revenue, in a format approved by the commissioner of revenue, when a qualified data center has met the requirements under paragraph (c) or a qualified refurbished data center has met the requirements under paragraph (d). The certification must provide the following information regarding each qualified data center or qualified refurbished data center:</p>
          <p>(1) the total square footage amount;</p>
          <p>(2) the total amount of construction or refurbishment costs and the total amount of qualifying investments in enterprise information technology equipment and computer software;</p>
          <p>(3) the beginning and ending of the applicable period under either paragraph (c) or (d) in which the qualifying expenditures and purchases under clause (2) were made, but in no case shall the period begin before July 1, 2012; and</p>
          <p>(4) the date upon which the qualified data center first met the requirements under paragraph (c) or a qualified refurbished data center first met the requirements under paragraph (d).</p>
          <p>(j) Any refund for sales tax paid on qualifying purchases under this subdivision must not be issued unless the commissioner of revenue has received the certification required under paragraph (i) issued by the commissioner of employment and economic development.</p>
          <p>(k) The commissioner of employment and economic development must annually notify the commissioner of revenue of the qualified data centers that are projected to meet the requirements under paragraph (c) and the qualified refurbished data centers that are projected to meet the requirements under paragraph (d) in each of the next four years. The notification must provide the information required under paragraph (i), clauses (1) to (4), for each qualified data center or qualified refurbished data center.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.68.43">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 43.<span class="headnote">Resold admission tickets.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) When a ticket reseller who purchased a ticket from a seller who is in the business of selling tickets resells the ticket, the ticket reseller must charge tax on the total amount for which the ticket is resold and the following rules apply:</p>
