          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 30.</h2>
          <p>MS 2006 [Expired]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.31">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 31.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/257/#laws.0.23.0">2006 c 257 s 23</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.32">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 32.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/308/#laws.9.94.0">2014 c 308 art 9 s 94</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.33">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 33.</h2>
          <p>MS 2008 [Expired, 2005 1Sp2003 art 5 s 14; 2006 c 259 art 3 s 5]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.34">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 34.<span class="headnote">Waste recovery facility.</span></h2>
          <p>Materials and supplies used or consumed in, and equipment incorporated into, the construction, improvement, or expansion of a waste-to-energy resource recovery facility are exempt if the facility uses biomass or mixed municipal solid waste as a primary fuel to generate steam or electricity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.35">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 35.<span class="headnote">Municipal utilities.</span></h2>
          <p>Materials and supplies used or consumed in, and equipment incorporated into, the construction, improvement, or expansion of electric generation and related facilities used pursuant to a joint power purchase agreement to meet the biomass energy mandate in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/216B.2424">216B.2424</a> are exempt if the owner or owners of the facilities are a municipal electric utility or utilities or a joint venture of municipal electric utilities. The tax must be imposed and collected as if the rate under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.62#stat.297A.62.1">297A.62, subdivision 1</a>, applied and then refunded under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.75">297A.75</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.36">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 36.</h2>
          <p>MS 2006 [Expired]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.37">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 37.</h2>
          <p>MS 2008 [Expired, 2006 c 247 s 12]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.38">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 38.</h2>
          <p>MS 2012 [Expired]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.39">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 39.</h2>
          <p>MS 2008 [Expired, 2006 c 259 art 3 s 2; 2010 c 389 art 4 s 7]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.40">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 40.<span class="headnote">Construction materials; Central Corridor light rail transit.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Materials and supplies used or consumed in, and equipment incorporated into, the construction or improvement of the Central Corridor light rail transit line and associated facilities including, but not limited to, stations, park-and-ride facilities, and maintenance facilities, are exempt. The tax must be imposed and collected as if the rate under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.62">297A.62</a>, subdivision 1, applied and then refunded in the manner provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.75">297A.75</a>. Refunds must not be applied for or issued until after July 1, 2009.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.41">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 41.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/308/#laws.9.94.0">2014 c 308 art 9 s 94</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.42">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 42.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/158/#laws.1.215.0">2016 c 158 art 1 s 215</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.43">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 43.</h2>
          <p>MS 2020 [Expired, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/299/#laws.1.6.0">2012 c 299 art 1 s 6</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.44">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 44.<span class="headnote">Building materials, capital projects.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Materials and supplies used or consumed in and equipment incorporated into the construction or improvement of a capital project funded partially or wholly under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.9905">297A.9905</a> are exempt, provided that the project has a total construction cost of at least $40,000,000 within a 24-month period.</p>
          <p>(b) Materials and supplies used or consumed in and equipment incorporated into the construction, remodeling, expansion, or improvement of an ice arena or other buildings or facilities owned and operated by the city of Plymouth are exempt. For purposes of this paragraph, "facilities" include municipal streets and facilities associated with streets including but not limited to lighting, curbs and gutters, and sidewalks. The total amount of refund on all building materials, supplies, and equipment that the city may apply for under this paragraph is $2,500,000.</p>
          <p>(c) The tax on purchases exempt under paragraph (a) and paragraph (b) must be imposed and collected as if the rate under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.62#stat.297A.62.1">297A.62, subdivision 1</a>, applied and then refunded in the manner provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.75">297A.75</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.45">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 45.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/85/#laws.0.115.0">2024 c 85 s 115</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.46">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 46.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/158/#laws.1.215.0">2016 c 158 art 1 s 215</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.47">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 47.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/158/#laws.1.215.0">2016 c 158 art 1 s 215</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.48">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 48.<span class="headnote">Construction materials, public infrastructure related to the destination medical center.</span></h2>
          <p>Materials and supplies used in, and equipment incorporated into, the construction and improvement of publicly owned buildings and infrastructure included in the development plan adopted under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/469.43">469.43</a>, and financed with public funds, are exempt.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.49">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 49.</h2>
          <p>MS 2020 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/55/#laws.1.187.0">2022 c 55 art 1 s 187</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.50">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 50.<span class="headnote">Properties destroyed by fire.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Building materials and supplies used in, and equipment incorporated into, the construction or replacement of real property that is located in Melrose affected by the fire on September 8, 2016, are exempt.</p>
          <p>(b) For sales and purchases made for the periods of (1) after September 30, 2016, and before July 1, 2017, and (2) after December 31, 2018, and before July 1, 2019, the tax must be imposed and collected as if the rate under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.62#stat.297A.62.1">297A.62, subdivision 1</a>, applied and then refunded in the manner provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.75">297A.75</a>.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.51">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 51.<span class="headnote">Properties destroyed by fire.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Building materials and supplies used or consumed in, and equipment incorporated into, the construction or replacement of real property affected by, and capital equipment to replace equipment destroyed in, the fire on March 11, 2018, in the city of Mazeppa are exempt. The tax must be imposed and collected as if the rate under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.62#stat.297A.62.1">297A.62, subdivision 1</a>, applied and then refunded in the manner provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.75">297A.75</a>. For purposes of this subdivision, "capital equipment" includes durable equipment used in a restaurant for food storage, preparation, and serving.</p>
          <p>(b) The exemption under this subdivision applies to sales and purchases made after March 11, 2018, and before January 1, 2025. Notwithstanding section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.40">289A.40</a>, a claim for refund may be filed until June 1, 2028.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.52">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 52.<span class="headnote">Construction; certain local government facilities.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Materials and supplies used in and equipment incorporated into the construction, reconstruction, upgrade, expansion, or remodeling of the following local government owned facilities are exempt:</p>
          <p>(1) a new fire station, which includes firefighting, emergency management, public safety training, and other public safety facilities in the city of Monticello if materials, supplies, and equipment are purchased after January 31, 2019, and before January 1, 2022;</p>
          <p>(2) a new fire station, which includes firefighting and public safety training facilities and public safety facilities, in the city of Inver Grove Heights if materials, supplies, and equipment are purchased after June 30, 2018, and before January 1, 2021;</p>
          <p>(3) a fire station and police station, including access roads, lighting, sidewalks, and utility components, on or adjacent to the property on which the fire station or police station are located that are necessary for safe access to and use of those buildings, in the city of Minnetonka if materials, supplies, and equipment are purchased after May 23, 2019, and before January 1, 2022;</p>
          <p>(4) the school building in Independent School District No. 414, Minneota, if materials, supplies, and equipment are purchased after January 1, 2018, and before January 1, 2021;</p>
          <p>(5) a fire station in the city of Mendota Heights, if materials, supplies, and equipment are purchased after December 31, 2018, and before January 1, 2021; and</p>
          <p>(6) a Dakota County law enforcement collaboration center, also known as the Safety and Mental Health Alternative Response Training (SMART) Center, if materials, supplies, and equipment are purchased after June 30, 2019, and before July 1, 2021.</p>
          <p>(b) The tax must be imposed and collected as if the rate under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.62">297A.62</a>, subdivision 1, applied and then refunded in the manner provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.75">297A.75</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) The total refund for the project listed in paragraph (a), clause (3), must not exceed $850,000.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.53">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 53.<span class="headnote">Public safety facilities.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Materials and supplies used or consumed in and equipment incorporated into the construction, remodeling, expansion, or improvement of a fire station or police station, including related facilities, owned and operated by a local government, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.70#stat.297A.70.2">297A.70, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (d), are exempt.</p>
          <p>(b) For purposes of this subdivision, "related facilities" includes access roads, lighting, sidewalks, and utility components on or adjacent to the property on which the fire station or police station is located that are necessary for safe access to and use of those buildings.</p>
          <p>(c) The tax must be imposed and collected as if the rate under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.62">297A.62</a>, subdivision 1, applied and then refunded in the manner provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.75">297A.75</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.71.54">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 54.<span class="headnote">Sustainable aviation fuel facilities.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Materials and supplies used or consumed in and equipment incorporated into the construction, reconstruction, or improvement of a facility located in Minnesota that produces or blends sustainable aviation fuel, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/41A.30#stat.41A.30.1">41A.30, subdivision 1</a>, is exempt.</p>
          <p>(b) The tax must be imposed and collected as if the rate under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.62">297A.62</a>, subdivision 1, applied and then refunded in the manner as provided for projects under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.75#stat.297A.75.1">297A.75, subdivision 1</a>, clause (1).</p>
          <p>(c) For a project, a portion of which is not used to produce or blend sustainable aviation fuel, the amount of purchases that are exempt under this subdivision must be determined by multiplying the total purchases, as specified in paragraph (a), by the ratio of:</p>
          <p>(1) the capacity to generate sustainable aviation fuel either through production or blending; and</p>
          <p>(2) the capacity to generate all fuels.</p>
          <p>(d) This subdivision expires July 1, 2034. The expiration does not affect refunds due for sales and purchases made prior to July 1, 2034.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.71.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.15.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 15</a>,44 subd 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/490/#laws.8.12.0">2000 c 490 art 8 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.12.65.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 12 s 65</a>-70; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/377/#laws.3.13.0">2002 c 377 art 3 s 13</a>-16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/397/#laws.0.2.0">2002 c 397 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/21/#laws.8.9.0">1Sp2003 c 21 art 8 s 9</a>,16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/56/#laws.0.1.0">2005 c 56 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/151/#laws.7.18.0">2005 c 151 art 7 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/3/#laws.5.14.0">1Sp2005 c 3 art 5 s 14</a>-17,32; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/247/#laws.0.12.0">2006 c 247 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/257/#laws.0.3.0">2006 c 257 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/259/#laws.3.2.0">2006 c 259 art 3 s 2</a>,5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/138/#laws.0.12.0">2007 c 138 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/366/#laws.7.4.0">2008 c 366 art 7 s 4</a>,5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/88/#laws.4.6.0">2009 c 88 art 4 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/389/#laws.4.6.0">2010 c 389 art 4 s 6</a>-8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/0/112/#laws.8.2.0">2011 c 112 art 8 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/299/#laws.1.6.0">2012 c 299 art 1 s 6</a>; art 5 s 2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/143/#laws.8.37.0">2013 c 143 art 8 s 37</a>-39; art 10 s 2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/1/#laws.3.30.0">1Sp2017 c 1 art 3 s 30</a>-32; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/182/#laws.1.84.0">2018 c 182 art 1 s 84</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/6/#laws.3.11.0">1Sp2019 c 6 art 3 s 11</a>-13; art 14 s 4; art 24 s 25; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/14/#laws.4.6.0">1Sp2021 c 14 art 4 s 6</a>,7,11; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/25/#laws.0.162.0">2023 c 25 s 162</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/64/#laws.5.10.0">2023 c 64 art 5 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/68/#laws.3.26.0">2023 c 68 art 3 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/85/#laws.0.93.0">2024 c 85 s 93</a>,94</p>

      <p><b>NOTE: </b>Subdivision 48, as added by Laws 2013, chapter 143, article 10, section 2, is effective for sales and purchases made after June 30, 2015, and before July 1, 2049. Laws 2013, chapter 143, article 10, section 2, the effective date.</p>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>Subdivision 50, paragraph (a), as added by Laws 2017, First Special Session chapter 1, article 3, section 32, is effective retroactively for sales and purchases made after September 30, 2016, and before July 1, 2023. Laws 2017, First Special Session chapter 1, article 3, section 32, the effective date, as amended by Laws 2019, First Special Session chapter 6, article 3, section 18, and Laws 2021, First Special Session chapter 14, article 4, section 11.</p>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>Subdivision 54, as added by Laws 2023, chapter 68, article 3, section 26, is effective for sales and purchases made after June 30, 2027, and before July 1, 2034. Laws 2023, chapter 68, article 3, section 26, the effective date.</p>
      <h1 class="fgh">EXEMPTION CERTIFICATES</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.72">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.72 EXEMPTION CERTIFICATES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.72.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/127/#laws.6.18.0">2003 c 127 art 6 s 18</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.72.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Content and form of exemption certificate.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">An exemption certificate must be substantially in the form prescribed by the commissioner. To be fully completed, the exemption certificate must:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) either be signed by the purchaser if it is a paper form, or meet the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.304">270C.304</a> if in electronic form;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) bear the name and address of the purchaser;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) indicate the identification number issued to the purchaser as follows:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) the purchaser's Minnesota tax identification number;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) if the purchaser does not have a Minnesota tax identification number, then the purchaser's state tax identification number that is issued by a state other than Minnesota, and the name of that state;</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) if the purchaser does not have an identification number described in either item (i) or (ii), then the purchaser's federal Employer Identification Number; or</p>
          <p class="in">(iv) if the purchaser does not have an identification number described in item (i), (ii), or (iii), then either the number of the purchaser's state-issued driver's license, if valid in the state of issue, or if the purchaser does not have a driver's license, a valid state-issued identification number, and the name of the state of issue;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) indicate the purchaser's type of business, using a business-type coding system prescribed by the commissioner; and</p>
          <p class="in">(5) indicate the reason for the exemption, using an exemption reason coding system prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.72.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Purchaser requirement.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A blanket exemption certificate is an exemption certificate used for continuing future purchases. A purchaser using a blanket exemption certificate must update it as needed to accurately reflect the information that is required under subdivision 2.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.72.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.16.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.12.71.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 12 s 71</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/151/#laws.2.17.0">2005 c 151 art 2 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/259/#laws.6.25.0">2006 c 259 art 6 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/154/#laws.12.35.0">2008 c 154 art 12 s 35</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.73">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.73 IMPROPER USE OF ITEM OBTAINED WITH EXEMPTION CERTIFICATE.</h1>
        <p>If a purchaser who gives an exemption certificate makes any use of the item that is not for a purpose exempted under this chapter, that use is considered a retail sale by the purchaser and the sales price to the purchaser is considered the gross receipts. If the sole use is rental while holding for sale, the purchaser shall include in the purchaser's gross receipts the amount of the rental charged. Upon subsequent sale of the item, the seller shall include the entire amount of gross receipts received from the sale without deduction of amounts previously received as rentals.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.73.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.17.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 17</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.74">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.74 COMMINGLING EXEMPTION CERTIFICATE ITEMS.</h1>
        <p>If a purchaser gives an exemption certificate for the purchase of fungible items and later commingles the items with similar fungible items not purchased exempt, sales from the commingled items are considered sales of items purchased exempt until a quantity has been sold that equals the quantity purchased exempt.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.74.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.18.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 18</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">EXEMPTION REFUNDS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.75">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.75 REFUND; APPROPRIATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.75.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Tax collected.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The tax on the gross receipts from the sale of the following exempt items must be imposed and collected as if the sale were taxable and the rate under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.62#stat.297A.62.1">297A.62, subdivision 1</a>, applied. The exempt items include:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) building materials for an agricultural processing facility exempt under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.71#stat.297A.71.13">297A.71, subdivision 13</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) building materials for mineral production facilities exempt under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.71#stat.297A.71.14">297A.71, subdivision 14</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) building materials for correctional facilities under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.71#stat.297A.71.3">297A.71, subdivision 3</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) building materials used in a residence for veterans with a disability exempt under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.71#stat.297A.71.11">297A.71, subdivision 11</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) elevators and building materials exempt under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.71#stat.297A.71.12">297A.71, subdivision 12</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) materials and supplies for qualified low-income housing under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.71#stat.297A.71.23">297A.71, subdivision 23</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) materials, supplies, and equipment for municipal electric utility facilities under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.71#stat.297A.71.35">297A.71, subdivision 35</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(8) equipment and materials used for the generation, transmission, and distribution of electrical energy and an aerial camera package exempt under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.68#stat.297A.68.37">297A.68, subdivision 37</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(9) commuter rail vehicle and repair parts under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.70#stat.297A.70.3">297A.70, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (a), clause (10);</p>
          <p class="in">(10) materials, supplies, and equipment for construction or improvement of projects and facilities under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.71#stat.297A.71.40">297A.71, subdivision 40</a>;</p>
          <p>(11) enterprise information technology equipment and computer software for use in a qualified data center exempt under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.68#stat.297A.68.42">297A.68, subdivision 42</a>;</p>
          <p>(12) materials, supplies, and equipment for qualifying capital projects under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.71#stat.297A.71.44">297A.71, subdivision 44</a>, paragraph (a), clause (1), and paragraph (b);</p>
          <p>(13) items purchased for use in providing critical access dental services exempt under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.70#stat.297A.70.7">297A.70, subdivision 7</a>, paragraph (c);</p>
          <p>(14) items and services purchased under a business subsidy agreement for use or consumption primarily in greater Minnesota exempt under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.68#stat.297A.68.44">297A.68, subdivision 44</a>;</p>
          <p>(15) building materials, equipment, and supplies for constructing or replacing real property exempt under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.71">297A.71, subdivisions 49</a>; 50, paragraph (b); and 51;</p>
          <p>(16) building materials, equipment, and supplies for qualifying capital projects under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.71#stat.297A.71.52">297A.71, subdivision 52</a>; and</p>
          <p>(17) building materials, equipment, and supplies for constructing, remodeling, expanding, or improving a fire station, police station, or related facilities exempt under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.71#stat.297A.71.53">297A.71, subdivision 53</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.75.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Refund; eligible persons.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Upon application on forms prescribed by the commissioner, a refund equal to the tax paid on the gross receipts of the exempt items must be paid to the applicant. Only the following persons may apply for the refund:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) for subdivision 1, clauses (1), (2), and (13), the applicant must be the purchaser;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) for subdivision 1, clause (3), the applicant must be the governmental subdivision;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) for subdivision 1, clause (4), the applicant must be the recipient of the benefits provided in United States Code, title 38, chapter 21;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) for subdivision 1, clause (5), the applicant must be the owner of the homestead property;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) for subdivision 1, clause (6), the owner of the qualified low-income housing project;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) for subdivision 1, clause (7), the applicant must be a municipal electric utility or a joint venture of municipal electric utilities;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) for subdivision 1, clauses (8), (11), and (14), the owner of the qualifying business;</p>
          <p class="in">(8) for subdivision 1, clauses (9), (10), (12), (16), and (17), the applicant must be the governmental entity that owns or contracts for the project or facility; and</p>
          <p class="in">(9) for subdivision 1, clause (15), the applicant must be the owner or developer of the building or project.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.75.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The application must include sufficient information to permit the commissioner to verify the tax paid. If the tax was paid by a contractor, subcontractor, or builder, under subdivision 1, clauses (3) to (12) or (14) to (17), the contractor, subcontractor, or builder must furnish to the refund applicant a statement including the cost of the exempt items and the taxes paid on the items unless otherwise specifically provided by this subdivision. The provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.40">289A.40</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.50">289A.50</a> apply to refunds under this section.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) An applicant may not file more than two applications per calendar year for refunds for taxes paid on capital equipment exempt under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.68#stat.297A.68.5">297A.68, subdivision 5</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.75.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Interest.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Interest must be paid on the refund at the rate in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.405">270C.405 </a>from 90 days after the refund claim is filed with the commissioner for taxes paid under subdivision 1.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.75.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Appropriation.</span></h2>
          <p class="in"> The amount required to make the refunds is annually appropriated to the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.75.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.19.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 19</a>,44 subd 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/490/#laws.8.6.0">2000 c 490 art 8 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.12.72.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 12 s 72</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/377/#laws.3.17.0">2002 c 377 art 3 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/127/#laws.1.26.0">2003 c 127 art 1 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/151/#laws.2.17.0">2005 c 151 art 2 s 17</a>; art 7 s 19; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/3/#laws.5.18.0">1Sp2005 c 3 art 5 s 18</a>-20; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/259/#laws.6.26.0">2006 c 259 art 6 s 26</a>-28; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/366/#laws.7.6.0">2008 c 366 art 7 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/88/#laws.4.7.0">2009 c 88 art 4 s 7</a>,8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/389/#laws.4.9.0">2010 c 389 art 4 s 9</a>-11; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/7/#laws.3.13.0">1Sp2011 c 7 art 3 s 13</a>-15; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/187/#laws.1.50.0">2012 c 187 art 1 s 50</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/294/#laws.2.18.0">2012 c 294 art 2 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/299/#laws.5.3.0">2012 c 299 art 5 s 3</a>-5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/143/#laws.8.40.0">2013 c 143 art 8 s 40</a>-42; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/308/#laws.9.73.0">2014 c 308 art 9 s 73</a>-75; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/1/#laws.3.33.0">1Sp2017 c 1 art 3 s 33</a>-36; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/6/#laws.3.14.0">1Sp2019 c 6 art 3 s 14</a>,15; art 24 s 26; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/83/#laws.1.74.0">2020 c 83 art 1 s 74</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/14/#laws.4.8.0">1Sp2021 c 14 art 4 s 8</a>-10; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/55/#laws.1.141.0">2022 c 55 art 1 s 141</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/85/#laws.0.95.0">2024 c 85 s 95</a>-97</p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">COMPUTATION AND COLLECTION OF SALES AND USE TAXES</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.76">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.76 COMPUTATION OF SALES AND USE TAXES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.76.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Rounding up or down.</span></h2>
          <p>In computing the sales or use tax to be collected or remitted as the result of a transaction, amounts of tax less than one-half of one cent must be disregarded and amounts of tax of one-half cent or more must be considered an additional cent.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.76.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Uniform tax collection methods; rules.</span></h2>
          <p>Agreements between competitive retailers or the adoption of appropriate rules or regulations by organizations or associations of retailers to provide uniform methods for adding the sales tax or its average equivalent, which do not involve otherwise unlawful price fixing agreements, are expressly authorized and are not in violation of any Minnesota laws prohibiting such agreements. The commissioner may prescribe rules for such agreements.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.76.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.20.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 20</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.77">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.77 COLLECTION OF SALES AND USE TAXES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.77.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Collection of tax at time of sale.</span></h2>
          <p>The tax must be stated and charged separately from the sales price insofar as practicable and must be collected by the seller from the purchaser.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.77.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Receipt.</span></h2>
          <p>For use tax, the retailer shall give the purchaser a tax receipt. The receipt must indicate the tax in the form of a notation on the sales slip or receipt for the sales price or in another form as prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.77.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Tax must be remitted.</span></h2>
          <p>The tax collected by a retailer under this section must be remitted to the commissioner as provided in chapter 289A and this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.77.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Status of sales and use taxes as debt.</span></h2>
          <p>Sales and use taxes that are required to be collected by a retailer are debts from the purchaser to the retailer recoverable at law in the same manner as other debts.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.77.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Records must be kept.</span></h2>
          <p>Every person liable for any tax imposed by this chapter, or for the collection thereof, shall keep such records, render such statements, make such returns, and comply with such rules, as the commissioner may from time to time prescribe.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.77.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.21.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.12.73.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 12 s 73</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/6/#laws.14.5.0">1Sp2019 c 6 art 14 s 5</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.78">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.78 LIABILITY FOR USE TAX; RECEIPT AS EVIDENCE.</h1>
        <p>Liability for the payment of the use tax is not extinguished until the tax has been paid to Minnesota. However, a receipt from a retailer given to the purchaser under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.77#stat.297A.77.2">297A.77, subdivision 2</a>, relieves the purchaser of further liability for the tax to which the receipt refers, unless the purchaser knows or has reason to know that the retailer did not have a permit to collect the tax.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.78.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.22.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 22</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.79">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.79 REPORTING OF GROSS RECEIPTS.</h1>
        <p>At the option of the taxpayer, gross receipts from sales may be reported on the cash basis as the consideration is received or on the accrual basis as sales are made.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.79.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.23.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 23</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.80">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.80 TAXES IN OTHER STATES; OFFSET AGAINST USE TAX.</h1>
        <p>If an article of tangible personal property or an item listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.63">297A.63</a> has already been taxed by another state and any subdivision thereof for its sale, storage, use, or other consumption in an amount less than the tax imposed by this chapter, then as to the person who paid the tax in the other state or any subdivision thereof, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.63">297A.63</a> applies only at a rate measured by the difference between the rate imposed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.62">297A.62</a> and the rate by which the previous tax was computed. If the tax imposed in the other state or any subdivision thereof is equal to or greater than the tax imposed in this state, then no tax is due from that person under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.63">297A.63</a>. The credit shall be applied first against the amount of any use tax due the state, and any unused portion of the credit shall then be applied against any use tax due a subdivision.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.80.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.24.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.12.74.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 12 s 74</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.81">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.81 UNCOLLECTIBLE DEBTS; OFFSET AGAINST OTHER TAXES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.81.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">General.</span></h2>
          <p>The taxpayer may offset against the taxes payable for any reporting period the amount of taxes imposed by this chapter previously paid as a result of any transaction the consideration for which became a debt owed to the taxpayer that became uncollectible during the reporting period, but only in proportion to the portion of the debt that became uncollectible. Section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.40#stat.289A.40.2">289A.40, subdivision 2</a>, applies to an offset under this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.81.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Manner of allowing deduction for uncollectible debt.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Uncollectible debt is allowed as a deduction in the manner provided in this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(b) If the uncollectible debt arose with respect to a sale required to be included in gross receipts, subject to a tax imposed under chapter 297A, the entire amount of the debt remaining uncollected is allowed as a deduction.</p>
          <p>(c) If the uncollectible debt arose with respect to a sale partly subject to the tax imposed under chapter 297A and partly exempt, the amount of the uncollectible debt allowed as a deduction is the amount derived by multiplying the uncollectible debt by the percentage that the taxable sale bears to the total sales.</p>
          <p>(d) If the uncollectible debt arose with respect to two or more sales made at successive intervals, payments made before the date the debt became uncollectible must be applied first to the earliest sale upon which there is an unpaid balance, and to following sales in successive order.</p>
          <p>(e) If the books and records of the taxpayer claiming the bad debt allowance support an allocation of the bad debts among the member states of the streamlined sales and use tax agreement, such an allocation shall be allowed.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.81.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Certified service provider.</span></h2>
          <p>A certified service provider, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.995#stat.297A.995.2">297A.995, subdivision 2</a>, on behalf of a taxpayer who is its client, may offset against taxes as provided by this section.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.81.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.25.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/127/#laws.1.27.0">2003 c 127 art 1 s 27</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.815">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.815 MOTOR VEHICLE LEASES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.815.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Motor vehicle lease price; payment.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) In the case of a lease of a motor vehicle as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.61#stat.297A.61.4">297A.61, subdivision 4</a>, paragraph (k), clause (2), the tax is imposed on the total amount to be paid by the lessee under the lease agreement. The lessor shall collect the tax in full at the time the lease is executed or, if the tax is included in the lease and the lease is assigned, the tax is due from the original lessor at the time the lease is assigned. The total amount to be paid by the lessee under the lease agreement equals the agreed-upon value of the vehicle less manufacturer's rebates, the stated residual value of the leased vehicle, and the total value allowed for a vehicle owned by the lessee taken in trade by the lessor, plus the price of any taxable goods and services included in the lease and the rent charge as provided by Code of Federal Regulations, title 12, section 213.4, excluding any rent charge related to the capitalization of the tax.</p>
          <p>(b) If the total amount paid by the lessee for use of the leased vehicle includes amounts that are not calculated at the time the lease is executed, the tax is imposed and must be collected by the lessor at the time the amounts are paid by the lessee. In the case of a lease which by its terms may be renewed, the sales tax is due and payable on the total amount to be paid during the initial term of the lease, and then for each subsequent renewal period on the total amount to be paid during the renewal period.</p>
          <p>(c) If a lease is canceled or rescinded on or before 90 days of its execution or if a vehicle is returned to the manufacturer under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/325F.665">325F.665</a>, the lessor may file a claim for a refund of the total tax paid minus the amount of tax due for the period the vehicle is used by the lessee.</p>
          <p>(d) If a lessee's obligation to make payments on a lease is canceled more than 90 days after its execution, a credit is allowed against sales tax or motor vehicles sales tax due on a subsequent lease or purchase of a motor vehicle if that lease or purchase is consummated within 30 days of the date the prior lease was canceled. The amount of the credit is equal to (1) the sales tax paid at the inception of the lease, multiplied by (2) the ratio of the number of full months remaining in the lease at the time of termination compared to the term of the lease used in calculating sales tax paid at the inception of the lease. The credit or any part of it cannot be assigned or transferred to another person.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.815.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Lease originating in another state.</span></h2>
          <p>When the lease of a motor vehicle as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.61#stat.297A.61.4">297A.61, subdivision 4</a>, paragraph (k), clause (2), originates in another state, the sales tax under subdivision 1 shall be calculated by the lessor on the total amount that is due under the lease agreement after the vehicle is required to be registered in Minnesota. If the total amount to be paid by the lessee under the lease agreement has already been subjected to tax by another state, a credit for taxes paid in the other state is allowed as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.80">297A.80</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.815.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Motor vehicle lease sales tax revenue.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) On or before June 30 of each fiscal year, the commissioner of revenue must estimate the revenues, including interest and penalties and minus refunds, collected under this section for the current fiscal year.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) By July 15 of the subsequent fiscal year, the commissioner of management and budget must transfer the revenues estimated under paragraph (a) from the general fund as follows:</p>
          <p>(1) 38 percent to the county state-aid highway fund;</p>
          <p>(2) 38 percent to the greater Minnesota transit account;</p>
          <p>(3) 13 percent to the local bridge program account in the special revenue fund, which is hereby created; and</p>
          <p>(4) 11 percent to the highway user tax distribution fund.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Notwithstanding any other law to the contrary, the commissioner of transportation must allocate the funds transferred under paragraph (b), clause (1), to the counties in the metropolitan area, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/473.121#stat.473.121.4">473.121, subdivision 4</a>, excluding the counties of Hennepin and Ramsey, so that each county receives the percentage that its population, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/477A.011#stat.477A.011.3">477A.011, subdivision 3</a>, estimated or established by July 15 of the year prior to the current calendar year, bears to the total population of the counties receiving funds under this paragraph.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) Money in the local bridge program account under paragraph (b), clause (3), is appropriated to the commissioner of transportation for the local bridge program under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/174.50">174.50, subdivisions 6</a> to 7.</p>
          <p>(e) The revenues under this subdivision do not include the revenues, including interest and penalties and minus refunds, generated by the sales tax imposed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.62#stat.297A.62.1a">297A.62, subdivision 1a</a>, which must be deposited as provided under the Minnesota Constitution, article XI, section 15.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.815.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/3/#laws.5.21.0">1Sp2005 c 3 art 5 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/259/#laws.6.29.0">2006 c 259 art 6 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/152/#laws.3.8.0">2008 c 152 art 3 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/216/#laws.0.16.0">2010 c 216 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/117/#laws.3.24.0">2013 c 117 art 3 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.11.29.0">2014 c 312 art 11 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/3/#laws.3.109.0">1Sp2017 c 3 art 3 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.3.92.0">2024 c 127 art 3 s 92</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.8155">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.8155 LIQUOR REPORTING REQUIREMENTS; PENALTY.</h1>
        <p class="in">A person who sells liquor, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/295.75#stat.295.75.1">295.75, subdivision 1</a>, in Minnesota to a retailer that sells liquor, shall file with the commissioner an annual informational report, in the form and manner prescribed by the commissioner, indicating the name, address, and Minnesota business identification number of each retailer, and the total dollar amount of liquor sold to each retailer in the previous calendar year. The report must be filed on or before March 31 following the close of the calendar year. A person failing to file this report is subject to the penalty imposed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.60">289A.60</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.8155.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/154/#laws.12.36.0">2008 c 154 art 12 s 36</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">AIRCRAFT AND FLIGHT EQUIPMENT</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.82">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.82 AIRCRAFT; FLIGHT EQUIPMENT; PAYMENT OF TAXES; EXEMPTIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.82.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Requirements for registration.</span></h2>
          <p>An aircraft must not be registered or licensed in this state unless the applicant presents proof that the sales or use tax imposed by this chapter has been paid or that the transaction is exempt from the sales and use tax. The exemption for an occasional sale under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.67#stat.297A.67.23">297A.67, subdivision 23</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.68#stat.297A.68.25">297A.68, subdivision 25</a>, does not apply to the sale or purchase of an aircraft.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.82.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Payment of tax to dealer.</span></h2>
          <p>If an aircraft is purchased from a dealer holding a valid sales and use tax permit under this chapter, the applicant shall present proof that the tax has been paid to the dealer.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.82.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Payment of tax.</span></h2>
          <p>If an aircraft is purchased from a person who is not the holder of a valid sales and use tax permit under this chapter, the purchaser shall pay the tax prior to registering or licensing the aircraft in this state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.82.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Exemptions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The following transactions are exempt from the tax imposed in this chapter to the extent provided.</p>
          <p>(b) The purchase or use of aircraft previously registered in Minnesota by a corporation or partnership is exempt if the transfer constitutes a transfer within the meaning of section 351 or 721 of the Internal Revenue Code.</p>
          <p>(c) The sale to or purchase, storage, use, or consumption by a licensed aircraft dealer of an aircraft for which a commercial use permit has been issued pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/360.654">360.654 </a>is exempt, if the aircraft is resold while the permit is in effect.</p>
          <p>(d) Air flight equipment when sold to, or purchased, stored, used, or consumed by airline companies, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270.071#stat.270.071.4">270.071, subdivision 4</a>, is exempt. For purposes of this subdivision, "air flight equipment" includes airplanes and parts necessary for the repair and maintenance of such air flight equipment, and flight simulators, but does not include aircraft with a maximum takeoff weight of less than 30,000 pounds.</p>
          <p>(e) Sales of, and the storage, distribution, use, or consumption of aircraft, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/360.511">360.511</a> and approved by the Federal Aviation Administration, and which the seller delivers to a purchaser outside Minnesota or which, without intermediate use, is shipped or transported outside Minnesota by the purchaser are exempt, but only if the purchaser is not a resident of Minnesota and provided that the aircraft is not thereafter returned to a point within Minnesota, except in the course of interstate commerce or isolated and occasional use, and will be registered in another state or country upon its removal from Minnesota. This exemption applies even if the purchaser takes possession of the aircraft in Minnesota and uses the aircraft in the state exclusively for training purposes for a period not to exceed ten days prior to removing the aircraft from this state.</p>
          <p>(f) The sale or purchase of the following items that relate to aircraft operated under Federal Aviation Regulations, Parts 91 and 135, and associated installation charges: equipment and parts necessary for repair and maintenance of aircraft; and equipment and parts to upgrade and improve aircraft.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.82.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Deposit in state airports fund.</span></h2>
          <p>Tax revenue, including interest and penalties, collected from the sale or purchase of an aircraft taxable under this chapter must be deposited in the state airports fund established in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/360.017">360.017</a>. For purposes of this subdivision, "revenue" does not include the revenue, including interest and penalties, generated by the sales tax imposed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.62#stat.297A.62.1a">297A.62, subdivision 1a</a>, which must be deposited as provided under article XI, section 15, of the Minnesota Constitution.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.82.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Exempt purchase certificate.</span></h2>
          <p>If the purchase of an aircraft is exempt under this chapter, the commissioner shall issue a certificate that no sales or use tax is due and owing in respect to the transaction.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.82.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Sales and leases; tax treatment.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A sale of aircraft and parts for the repair of aircraft purchased by a nonprofit, incorporated flying club or association utilized solely by the corporation by leasing the aircraft to shareholders of the corporation is exempt as property purchased for resale. The leasing of the aircraft to the shareholders by the flying club or association is taxable as a retail sale.</p>
          <p>(b) A lease of aircraft utilized by a lessee for leasing to others, whether or not the lessee also utilizes the aircraft for charter service or for flight instruction if no separate charge is made for aircraft rental, is exempt as a purchase for resale. However, a proportionate share of the lease payment reflecting use for flight instruction or charter service is taxable under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.63">297A.63</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.82.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Agreement with commissioner of transportation.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding subdivisions 1 to 4, the commissioner may enter into an agreement with the commissioner of transportation whereby, upon approval of both commissioners, the commissioner of transportation will collect the sales tax on aircraft from persons required to register or license aircraft in this state. For purposes of collecting the tax, the commissioner of transportation shall act as agent of the commissioner of revenue and shall be subject to all rules not inconsistent with the provisions of this chapter, that may be prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.82.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.26.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 26</a>,44 subd 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/490/#laws.8.7.0">2000 c 490 art 8 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.7.53.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 7 s 53</a>,54; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/143/#laws.5.4.0">2013 c 143 art 5 s 4</a>,5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/1/#laws.14.9.0">1Sp2017 c 1 art 14 s 9</a>,10</p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">SNOWMOBILES, ALL-TERRAIN VEHICLES, AND WATERCRAFT</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.825">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.825 SNOWMOBILES; ALL-TERRAIN VEHICLES; WATERCRAFT; PAYMENT OF TAXES; REFUNDS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.825.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Agreement with commissioners of natural resources and public safety; collection and refunds.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may enter into an agreement with the commissioner of natural resources, in consultation with the commissioner of public safety, that provides that:</p>
          <p>(1) the commissioner of natural resources and authorized deputy registrars of motor vehicles must collect use tax on snowmobiles, all-terrain vehicles, and watercraft from persons applying for initial registration or license of the item unless the applicant provides a receipt, invoice, or other document to prove that:</p>
          <p>(i) sales tax was paid on the purchase;</p>
          <p>(ii) the purchase was exempt under this chapter;</p>
          <p>(iii) use tax was paid to the commissioner in a form prescribed by the commissioner; or</p>
          <p>(iv) the item was purchased from a retailer that is maintaining a place of business in this state as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.66#stat.297A.66.1">297A.66, subdivision 1</a>, and is a dealer as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/84.81#stat.84.81.10">84.81, subdivision 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/84.92#stat.84.92.3">84.92, subdivision 3</a>; or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/86B.005#stat.86B.005.4">86B.005, subdivision 4</a>; and</p>
          <p>(2) the commissioner of natural resources and authorized deputy registrars of motor vehicles are authorized to issue refunds of use tax paid to them in error, meaning that either the sales or use tax had already been paid or that the purchase was exempt from tax under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.825.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Agents.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of collecting or refunding the tax under this section, the commissioner of natural resources and authorized deputy registrars of motor vehicles are the agents of the commissioner and are subject to, and must strictly comply with, all rules consistent with this chapter prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.825.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/1/#laws.21.7.0">1Sp2017 c 1 art 21 s 7</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">PERMITS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.83">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.83 APPLICATION FOR PERMIT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.83.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Persons applying.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A retailer required to collect and remit sales taxes under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.66">297A.66</a> shall file with the commissioner an application for a permit.</p>
          <p>(b) A retailer making retail sales from outside this state to a destination within this state who is not required to obtain a permit under paragraph (a) may nevertheless voluntarily file an application for a permit.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner may require any person or class of persons obligated to file a use tax return under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.11#stat.289A.11.3">289A.11, subdivision 3</a>, to file an application for a permit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.83.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Application requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>The application must be made on a form prescribed by the commissioner and indicate the name under which the applicant intends to transact business, the location of the applicant's place or places of business, and other information the commissioner may require. The application must be filed by the owner, if a natural person; by a member or partner, if the owner is an association or partnership; or by a person authorized to file the application, if the owner is a corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.83.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Commissioner's discretion.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner may decline to issue a permit to a retailer not maintaining a place of business in this state, or may cancel a permit previously issued to the retailer, if the commissioner believes that the tax can be collected more effectively from the persons using the property in this state. A refusal to issue or cancellation of a permit on such grounds does not affect the retailer's right to make retail sales from outside this state to destinations within this state.</p>
          <p>(b) If the commissioner considers it necessary for the efficient administration of the tax to regard a salesperson, representative, trucker, peddler, or canvasser as the agent of the dealer, distributor, supervisor, employer, or other person under whom that person operates or from whom the person obtains the tangible personal property sold, whether making sales personally or in behalf of that dealer, distributor, supervisor, employer, or other person, the commissioner may regard the salesperson, representative, trucker, peddler, or canvasser as such agent, and may regard the dealer, distributor, supervisor, employer, or other person as a retailer for the purposes of collecting the tax.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.83.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.27.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 27</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.84">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.84 PERMITS ISSUED AND NOT ISSUED; CANCELLATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.84.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The following definitions apply for the purposes of this section.</p>
          <p>(b) "Applicant" means an individual, corporation, or partnership. Applicant also includes any officer of a corporation or member of a partnership.</p>
          <p>(c) "Delinquent sales tax" means tax not paid by the date the tax was due and payable under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.20#stat.289A.20.4">289A.20, subdivision 4</a>, or an assessment not paid if the applicant has been issued an order assessing sales and use tax under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.33#stat.270C.33.4">270C.33, subdivision 4</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.84.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Permits issued.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as provided in subdivision 3, the commissioner must issue a permit to each applicant who has complied with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.83">297A.83</a>, and with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.92">297A.92</a> if security is required. A person is considered to have a permit if the person has a Minnesota tax identification number issued by the commissioner that is currently active for taxes imposed by this chapter. A permit is valid until canceled or revoked. It is not assignable and is valid only for the person in whose name it is granted and for the transaction of business at the places designated on the permit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.84.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Permits not issued.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except as provided in paragraph (b), the commissioner must not issue a permit to an applicant if the applicant is liable for delinquent sales tax.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner must issue a permit to an applicant if an appeal period of an order assessing sales tax under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.33#stat.270C.33.5">270C.33, subdivision 5</a>, has not ended. The commissioner may cancel a permit issued under this paragraph in the manner provided in subdivision 4 if the applicant owes delinquent sales tax after the appeal period has ended.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.84.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Nonconforming permits; cancellation; reissue.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If the commissioner issues a permit that does not conform with the requirements of this section or applicable rules, the commissioner may cancel the permit upon notice to the permit holder. The notice must be served by first class and certified mail at the permit holder's last known address. The cancellation is effective immediately.</p>
          <p>(b) If a permit holder shows that a canceled permit was issued in conformance with the requirements of this section and applicable rules, the commissioner must reissue the permit.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.84.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.28.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/158/#laws.1.214.0">2016 c 158 art 1 s 214</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/6/#laws.23.2.0">1Sp2019 c 6 art 23 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.85">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.85 CANCELLATION OF PERMITS.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner may cancel a permit if one of the following conditions occurs:</p>
        <p>(1) the permit holder has not filed a sales or use tax return for at least one year;</p>
        <p>(2) the permit holder has not reported any sales or use tax liability on the permit holder's returns for at least two years;</p>
        <p>(3) the permit holder requests cancellation of the permit;</p>
        <p>(4) the permit is subject to cancellation under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.722#stat.270C.722.2">270C.722, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (a);</p>
        <p>(5) the permit is subject to cancellation under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.84">297A.84</a>; or</p>
        <p>(6) the permit holder is a taxable cannabis product retailer as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/295.81">295.81</a>, subdivision 1, paragraph (s), other than a lower-potency hemp edible retailer as licensed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/342.43#stat.342.43.1">342.43, subdivision 1</a>, and its license to sell a taxable cannabis product as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/295.81#stat.295.81.1">295.81, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (r), has been revoked by the Office of Cannabis Management.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.85.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.29.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/127/#laws.8.12.0">2003 c 127 art 8 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/151/#laws.2.17.0">2005 c 151 art 2 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/6/#laws.23.3.0">1Sp2019 c 6 art 23 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/63/#laws.2.18.0">2023 c 63 art 2 s 18</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.297A.86"><b>297A.86</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/151/#laws.1.117.0">2005 c 151 art 1 s 117</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.87">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.87 FLEA MARKETS, SHOWS, AND OTHER SELLING EVENTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.87.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Events affected.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) This section applies to a flea market, craft show, antique show, coin show, stamp show, comic book show, convention exhibit area, or similar selling event.</p>
          <p>(b) To be subject to this section, the operator of an event described in paragraph (a) must rent or lease space on the sale premises to the seller, charge the seller a registration or participation fee, or receive a percentage of sales or other consideration from a seller as a condition to participation by a seller in the event.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.87.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Seller's permit or alternate statement.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The operator of an event under subdivision 1 shall obtain one of the following from a person who wishes to do business as a seller at the event:</p>
          <p>(1) evidence that the person holds a valid seller's permit under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.84">297A.84</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) a written statement that the person is not offering for sale any item that is taxable under this chapter; or</p>
          <p>(3) a written statement that this is the only selling event that the person will be participating in for that calendar year, that the person will be participating for three or fewer days, and that the person will make less than $500 in total sales in the calendar year. The written statement shall include the person's name, address, and telephone number.</p>
          <p>(b) The operator shall require the evidence or statement as a prerequisite to participating in the event as a seller.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.87.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Occasional sale provisions applicable under limited circumstances.</span></h2>
          <p>The isolated and occasional sale provision under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.67#stat.297A.67.23">297A.67, subdivision 23</a>, applies, provided that the seller only participates for three or fewer days in one event per calendar year, makes $500 or less in sales in the calendar year, and provides the written statement required in subdivision 2, paragraph (a), clause (3). The isolated and occasional sales provision under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.68#stat.297A.68.25">297A.68, subdivision 25</a>, does not apply to a seller at an event under this section.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.87.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.31.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/151/#laws.7.20.0">2005 c 151 art 7 s 20</a>,21</p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">DIRECT PAY</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.89">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.89 DIRECT PAYMENT BY PURCHASERS PERMITTED.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.89.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Commissioner may permit.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may permit purchasers to pay taxes imposed by this chapter directly to the commissioner. Any taxes paid by purchasers under this section are considered use taxes.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.89.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Retailer does not collect.</span></h2>
          <p>The retailer shall not collect the tax from a purchaser who furnishes to the retailer a fully completed exemption certificate as described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.72">297A.72</a>, indicating that the purchaser is authorized to pay any sales or use tax due on purchases made by the purchaser directly to the commissioner under subdivision 1.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.297A.89.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.32.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.12.76.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 12 s 76</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/0/112/#laws.8.3.0">2011 c 112 art 8 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.297A.90">
        <h1 class="shn">297A.90 INTERSTATE MOTOR CARRIERS AS RETAILERS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.90.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Registration; records.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A person who is engaged in interstate for-hire transportation of tangible personal property or passengers by motor vehicle may, under rules prescribed by the commissioner, register as a retailer and pay the taxes imposed by this chapter in accordance with this section. Any taxes paid under this section are use taxes.</p>
          <p>(b) As used in this section, "person" means:</p>
          <p>(1) one who possesses a certificate or permit or has completed a registration process that authorizes for-hire transportation of property or passengers from the United States Department of Transportation or the Department of Transportation;</p>
          <p>(2) one who transports commodities defined as "exempt" in for-hire transportation in interstate commerce; or</p>
          <p>(3) one who transports tangible personal property in interstate commerce, pursuant to contracts with persons described in clause (1) or (2).</p>
          <p class="bl">Persons qualifying under clause (2) or (3) must maintain on a current basis the same type of mileage records that are required by persons specified in clause (1) by the United States Department of Transportation.</p>
          <p>(c) Persons who in the course of their business are transporting solely their own goods in interstate commerce may also register as retailers under rules prescribed by the commissioner and pay the taxes imposed by this chapter in accordance with this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.297A.90.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Payment of tax.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Persons who are registered as retailers may make purchases in this state or import property into this state without payment of the sales or use taxes imposed by this chapter at the time of purchase or importation, if the purchases or importations come within the provisions of this section and are made in strict compliance with the rules of the commissioner.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) A person described in subdivision 1 may elect to pay directly to the commissioner any sales or use tax that may be due under this chapter for the acquisition of mobile transportation equipment and parts and accessories attached or to be attached to such equipment registered under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/168.187">168.187</a>.</p>
